Method: 提出EgoPriMo框架，核心是Triple-stream DiT（扩散Transformer），分别建模身体动力学（SMPL）、第一人称视觉上下文和文本语言。通过任务条件掩码（task-conditioning masks）实现同一checkpoint支持多种任务（重建、生成、预测）并处理缺失模态数据。使用第一人称数据集Nymeria和EgoExo4D训练。

Result: 在Nymeria和EgoExo4D上，单个checkpoint在运动生成任务上超越UniEgoMotion，同时支持重建和预测。生成的SMPL运动可被Unitree人形机器人控制器成功执行。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了从可扩展的第一人称观察到可泛化、可交互的人形机器人运动先验的实用路径，验证了统一框架处理多种运动任务的有效性，以及从人类演示到真实机器人部署的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require whole-body motions that adapt to scene context, task requirements, and user intent. Motion tracking reproduces specified trajectories, and humanoid vision-language-action systems provide semantic interfaces, but neither offers a scalable and interactive prior for broad full-body behavior. We introduce EgoPriMo (Egocentric Motion Prior for Humanoid Robots), a unified framework that learns such priors from egocentric human demonstrations. Given egocentric observations and a text prompt, EgoPriMo reconstructs, generates, and forecasts SMPL-based full-body motion. Language is used as a high-level control signal rather than a complete motion specification. At the core of EgoPriMo is a Triple-stream DiT that jointly models body dynamics, egocentric visual context, and text; task-conditioning masks route different tasks and missing-modality data through the same checkpoint. Experiments on Nymeria and EgoExo4D show that one checkpoint improves egocentric motion generation over UniEgoMotion while supporting reconstruction and forecasting; the generated SMPL motions can also be executed by a Unitree humanoid controller. These results indicate a practical path from scalable egocentric observations to generalizable and interactive humanoid motion priors.

</details>


### [254] [ActProbe: Action-Space Probe for Early Failure Detection of Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08508)
*Bingjia Huang,Xiangyu Li,Xiang Wang,Liang Mi,Zixu Hao,Weijun Wang,Hao Wu,Kun Li,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActProbe是一种轻量级纯动作空间失败检测器，通过单次前向传播中的时序一致性误差(TCE)和动作块幅度(ACM)两个信号，结合任务条件LSTM-MLP架构，在生成式机器人策略执行失败前即发出预警。在多个基准上F1-时效性帕累托前沿平均提升+12.7%，未见任务早期检测ROC-AUC提升+9.0%，并能在真实机器人上加速RL微调2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线失败检测器要么需要白盒访问策略内部，要么通过重采样和观测侧信号增加运行时开销，缺乏轻量级、高效的部署时失败预测方法。

Method: 提出ActProbe，利用生成式机器人策略输出的动作块本身包含失败预测信号：TCE（连续动作块间的时序一致性误差）和ACM（当前动作块幅度）。通过任务条件LSTM-MLP网络将这两个信号映射为每步失败概率。

Result: F1-时效性帕累托前沿平均超体积增益+12.7%，未见任务早期检测ROC-AUC领先+9.0%，真实机器人拾取任务上成功迁移，RL微调（PPO）减少2.9倍环境交互次数。

Conclusion: 纯动作空间的失败检测信号已足够有效，无需观测或策略内部信息即可实现高精度、早期且轻量的失败预测，且能直接迁移到真实部署和加速后续学习。

Abstract: Generative robot policies fail unpredictably at deployment: they hesitate at critical moments, drift off-task, or commit to unrecoverable actions. Existing online failure detectors either require white-box access to policy internals or add runtime overhead through resampling and observation-side signals. Our empirical analysis shows that emitted action chunks themselves already carry strong predictive signal for impending failures in generative robot policies. Motivated by this observation, we introduce ActProbe, a lightweight, pure action-space detector that uses two compact signals available from a single forward pass: Temporal Consistency Error (TCE) between consecutive action chunks and Action Chunk Magnitude (ACM) of the current chunk. ActProbe maps these signals to per-step failure probabilities with a task-conditioned LSTM-MLP architecture. Across a diverse suite of generative robot policies and benchmarks, ActProbe raises alerts before failures become visually recognizable, improving the accuracy (F1)-timeliness Pareto frontier of failure detection by an average hypervolume gain of +12.7% over both internal- and external-feature baselines, with a +9.0% early-detection ROC-AUC lead on unseen tasks. ActProbe further transfers to deployment, predicting failures on unseen real-robot pick tasks and accelerating RL fine-tuning (PPO) with 2.9x fewer environment interactions.

</details>


### [255] [Towards End to End Motion Planning and Execution for Autonomous Underwater Vehicles Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08513)
*Elisei Shafer,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了端到端深度强化学习（DRL）用于自主水下机器人（AUV）导航的可行性，提出分层强化学习（HRL）架构：高层策略（2Hz）处理原始传感器数据（单目相机、前视成像声纳、本体感知）生成空间子目标，低层策略（10Hz）将子目标映射为推进器指令。高层使用RLPD+SERL训练，低层使用SAC+HER。在HoloOcean仿真中，方法成功实现避障，轨迹长度接近RRT*基线（偏差4%-6%），对传感器噪声和能见度下降具鲁棒性，但在未见区域泛化有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AUV依赖复杂的手工工程化感知-规划-控制管线，本文希望探索端到端DRL方法减少人工工程，实现从原始传感器数据直接到推进器指令的映射。

Method: 提出分层强化学习（HRL）架构：高层策略（2Hz）处理堆叠的84×84单目图像、100×100前视声纳和本体感知数据，使用RLPD+SERL训练生成子目标；低层策略（10Hz）使用SAC+HER将子目标转换为推进器指令。

Result: 在HoloOcean仿真中成功实现避障，轨迹长度接近RRT*基线（偏差4%-6%），对传感器噪声和能见度下降具有鲁棒性，但泛化到未知障碍物形状时存在局限。

Conclusion: 端到端DRL方法在水下导航中具有潜力，能够在最小计算硬件上实现样本高效的自主导航，但需要进一步提升对全新场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) traditionally rely on complex, heavily engineered pipelines for perception, path planning, and motion control. This paper explores the feasibility of an end-to-end Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) approach that maps raw sensor data directly to thruster commands, reducing manual engineering. We propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) architecture splitting the problem into two Markov Decision Processes. A High-Level (HL) policy operating at 2Hz processes raw $84 \times 84$ pixel monocular camera frames, stacked $100 \times 100$ pixel forward-looking imaging sonar, and proprioceptive data to generate spatial subgoals. Simultaneously, a Low-Level (LL) policy operating at 10Hz converts these subgoals into thruster commands. The HL policy is trained using Reinforcement Learning from Prior Demonstrations (RLPD) within a modified Sample-Efficient Robotic Reinforcement Learning (SERL) framework, while the LL policy utilizes Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) combined with Hindsight Experience Replay (HER). Evaluated in the high-fidelity HoloOcean simulator, our method demonstrates successful obstacle avoidance, achieving trajectory lengths closely approximating (within 4% to 6% of) an $\text{RRT}^*$ planning baseline. Furthermore, the learned policy exhibits strong robustness to simulated sensor noise and decreased visibility. While the system navigates familiar geometries effectively, experiments reveal generalization limitations when encountering unvisited areas with novel obstacle shapes. Ultimately, this work demonstrates the promise of sample-efficient, end-to-end DRL for underwater navigation using minimal computational hardware.

</details>


### [256] [Two Bridges, One Pathway: From VLMs to Generalizable VLAs with Embodied Trajectory-Coupled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08520)
*Linqi Yin,Shiduo Zhang,Shenling Qiu,Chenxin Li,Zhaoyang Fu,Lei Xiao,Xiang Wang,Chenchen Yang,Zhe Xu,Pengfang Qian,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Xuanjing Huang,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段训练策略（分布桥接、目标桥接、保留适应），通过引入"具身轨迹对齐（ETC）数据"作为中间步骤，逐步弥合VLM预训练与VLA动作学习之间的双重鸿沟（视觉语义鸿沟+目标鸿沟），从而将VLM的泛化能力有效迁移为可部署的机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM虽然具备强大的通用推理能力，但直接微调为VLA存在双重鸿沟：VLM基于互联网图像和语言理解目标训练，而VLA需要感知机器人场景并预测动作。直接微调导致学习曲线陡峭，预训练泛化能力退化而非迁移。

Method: 提出具身轨迹对齐（ETC）数据（与动作数据同源的视觉-语言监督数据），设计三阶段训练策略：(1) 分布桥接——适配VLM到具身视觉-语言语义；(2) 目标桥接——逐步向动作预测过渡；(3) 保留适应——在目标部署域特化策略。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验验证了渐进桥接策略是将VLM泛化能力迁移为鲁棒、可部署机器人策略的关键。混合任务相关分布外ETC数据与少量动作数据，能使模型泛化到新的视觉-语言条件，无需额外机器人演示。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的中间数据（ETC）和多阶段渐进训练，可以有效弥合VLM到VLA的鸿沟，实现VLM泛化能力的有效迁移。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are powerful general-purpose reasoners, yet converting them into robot control policies (VLAs) is surprisingly difficult. The root cause is a two-fold gap: VLMs are trained on internet-scale images with language-understanding objectives, while VLAs must perceive robot scenes and predict motor actions. Fine-tuning a VLM directly on robot action data forces the model to cross both gaps at once -- the learning curve is steep and the rich generalizations learned during pretraining tend to degrade rather than transfer. We argue that this gap can be bridged gradually with the right intermediate data. We introduce \emph{embodied trajectory-coupled (ETC) data} -- vision-language supervision derived from the same robot scenes and trajectories used for action learning. Because ETC data shares the visual context of robot operation while retaining familiar language-understanding objectives, it provides a natural stepping stone between VLM pretraining and VLA fine-tuning. Building on this, we design a three-stage training recipe. Distribution Bridging first adapts the VLM to embodied visual-language semantics. Objective Bridging then gradually shifts the model toward action prediction while preserving the acquired representations. Retentive Adaptation finally specializes the policy to the target deployment domain. We further show that mixing task-relevant out-of-distribution ETC data with a small amount of action data enables the model to generalize to novel visual-language conditions without requiring additional robot demonstrations. Simulation and real-robot experiments confirm that this gradual bridging strategy is the key to transferring VLM generalization into robust, deployable robot policies.

</details>


### [257] [GEAR-VLA: Learning Geometry-Aware Action Representations for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08530)
*Yuan Zhang,Shiqi Zhang,Yedong Shen,Shuai Dong,Jiajun Deng,Xin Zhang,Yuxuan Gao,Jiajia Wu,Xin Nie,Zhiyuan Cheng,Jianmin Ji,Yanyong Zhang,Xingyi Zhang,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GEAR-VLA提出了一种统一的几何感知动作表示框架，通过粗到精的动作学习、语义对齐的3D集成和具身规范化，解决了现有VLA模型在未见过物体、背景偏移和不同机器人本体上的泛化问题，在多个基准和真实实验中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在真实部署中面对未见物体、背景变化和不同机器人本体时表现不佳，根本原因在于缺乏统一的几何感知操控表示，导致低层轨迹监督不对齐、3D特征错位以及本体差异无法处理。

Method: 1) 粗到精动作学习：多源具身预训练赋予VLM具身推理和离散动作理解能力，潜在动作令牌连接梯度解耦的DiT连续动作专家；2) 语义对齐3D集成：可训练的3D空间骨干与VLA表示对齐，冻结原始VLM对齐的视觉通路；3) 具身规范化：具身感知状态与具身不变动作将机器人差异限制在低层接口。

Result: 在LIBERO、零样本LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0上达到SOTA；AgileX上85.9%成功率，预训练未见LDT-01本体上81.0%；6,360次通用抓取基准测试中212个未见物体达到90.1%成功率。

Conclusion: GEAR-VLA通过几何感知的通用动作表示有效提升了VLA在多种泛化场景下的操控能力，验证了3D空间对齐和本体规范化对跨本体迁移的关键作用。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong benchmark performance but still struggle in real-world deployment with unseen objects, background shifts, and different robot embodiments. We argue that this stems from the lack of a unified geometry-aware manipulation representation, leaving existing VLAs vulnerable to low-level trajectory supervision, misaligned 3D features, and embodiment differences. To address this, we propose GEAR-VLA, a VLA framework for learning unified geometry-aware action representations for generalizable robotic manipulation. GEAR-VLA adopts coarse-to-fine action learning, where multi-source embodied pretraining equips the VLM with embodied reasoning and discrete action understanding before latent action tokens connect action semantics to a gradient-decoupled DiT continuous action expert. It further performs semantic-aligned 3D integration by aligning a trainable 3D spatial backbone with the VLA representation while freezing the original VLM-aligned visual pathway. To share this representation across robots, GEAR-VLA uses embodiment canonicalization, where embodiment-aware states and embodiment-invariant actions confine robot differences to the low-level interface. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate strong generalization: GEAR-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO, zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin 2.0, reaches 85.9% success on AgileX and 81.0% on the pretraining-unseen LDT-01 embodiment, and obtains 90.1% success on a 6,360-trial universal grasping benchmark with 212 unseen objects. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/babynabeauty/GEAR-VLA.

</details>


### [258] [When Video Misreads: Closed-Loop Distillation of Reading Heuristics for Exploratory Manipulation Trace QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08542)
*Haizhou Ge,Yufei Jia,Yue Li,Zhixing Chen,Lu Shi,Lei Han,Guyue Zhou,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EMT-QA任务：机器人通过探索性操作轨迹（如拉锁住的抽屉失败）推断潜在前置条件，从而恢复最小成功动作链。引入闭环轨迹蒸馏（CLTD）方法，从训练轨迹中提取一句自然语言蒸馏阅读启发（DRH），辅助冻结的VLM在推理时正确解读轨迹。在模拟器和真实机器人任务上，DRH将动作链准确率提升0.38-0.47，且可作为一次性程序化分类器的唯一规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人探索性操作中，失败的尝试往往蕴含关键信息（如抽屉被锁），但现有VLM和多模态LLM无法从原始视觉、本体感知或它们的组合中正确解读这些线索，无法恢复最小成功动作链。

Method: 闭环轨迹蒸馏（CLTD）：每个任务使用编码agent检查标注的训练轨迹，蒸馏出一行自然语言提示（DRH）。推理时，冻结的VLM接收原始轨迹+DRH作为提示，无需调用agent或更新权重。

Result: 在三个模拟器和两个真实机器人任务上，DRH相比最佳原始模态基线，动作链准确率提升0.38-0.47。DRH还可作为一次性程序化分类器的唯一规范，性能与提示VLM相当。

Conclusion: 探索性操作轨迹中的失败信息可以被系统性地蒸馏为简洁的自然语言启发式规则，显著提升VLM在EMT-QA任务上的推理能力，且该规则具有良好的可迁移性和可解释性。

Abstract: Exploratory manipulation often turns an apparent failed attempt into the key evidence for what to do next. For example, a robot pulls a locked cabinet drawer, fails, and only succeeds after opening the lock. The failed pull reveals a latent precondition (the drawer is locked) that determines the minimal-success action chain (the fewest actions that complete the task), here [lock-open, drawer-pull]. Correctly reading this trace is therefore the prerequisite for recovering that chain. We formalize this setting as Exploratory Manipulation Trace QA (EMT-QA): given synchronized video and proprioception from an exploratory trace, predict the minimal-success action chain under the latent precondition revealed by the probe. However, even state-of-the-art VLMs and embodied multimodal LLMs misread this evidence: they do not reliably recover the chain from raw video, raw proprioception, or their combination.
  We introduce Closed-Loop Trace Distillation, a pipeline that uses a per-task coding agent to inspect labeled training traces and distill a one-line natural-language prompt over the trace, which we call the Distilled Reading Heuristic (DRH). At inference, no agent is invoked and no model weights are updated; a frozen VLM receives the raw trace plus the DRH as a prompt entry. Across three simulator and two real-robot tasks, the DRH improves chain accuracy by +0.38 to +0.47 over the best raw-modality baseline. The same DRH also serves as the sole specification for one-shot programmatic classifiers that match the prompted VLM.

</details>


### [259] [OASIS: From Simulation Data Collection to Real-World Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08548)
*Zehao Yu,Jiakun Zheng,Weiji Xie,Jiyuan Shi,Chenyun Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了OASIS框架，利用仿真数据驱动的人形机器人全身操控（loco-manipulation）。通过3D生成模型从真实图像重建物体资产，在仿真中收集遥操作轨迹并增强，训练分层视觉运动策略，实现零样本部署到真实人形机器人，性能超过真实遥操作数据训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人全身操控数据来源存在轨迹质量与可扩展性之间的矛盾：真实遥操作轨迹质量高但成本大，仿真数据可扩展但通常与真实世界有差距。作者希望利用仿真数据同时获得高质量、可扩展且对齐于机器人本体的数据。

Method: 1) 使用3D生成模型从真实世界图像自动重建物体资产；2) 在仿真中通过遥操作收集轨迹；3) 在后处理阶段对轨迹进行广泛的域随机化增强（光照、环境等）；4) 设计分层视觉运动策略用于人形机器人全身操控。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上零样本部署，使用仿真数据训练的策略在大多数任务上成功率高于使用真实遥操作数据训练的策略，主要归因于仿真渲染中覆盖的广泛光照和环境变化。

Conclusion: OASIS证明了通过仿真数据生成可以有效解决人形机器人操控中的数据困境，仿真数据经过域随机化后甚至能超越真实数据训练的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent progress in robot manipulation has been largely driven by learning from large-scale demonstrations. For humanoid robot loco-manipulation tasks, however, existing data sources force an unsatisfying tradeoff between trajectory quality and scalability. Real-world teleoperation provides the highest-quality trajectories but requires dedicated physical space and time-consuming scene resets. Simulation offers an alternative way out of this dilemma: it can produce clean, embodiment-aligned data at scale without any physical hardware. In this paper, we propose OASIS, a simulation-data-driven framework for humanoid loco-manipulation. OASIS automatically reconstructs realistic object assets from real-world images using a 3D generative model. Based on these assets, trajectories are first collected through teleoperation in simulation, and then augmented under diverse domain randomizations in a post-processing stage. With the resulting simulation data, we further design a hierarchical visuomotor policy for humanoid loco-manipulation. Extensive experiments on the real humanoid robot show that, under zero-shot deployment, the policy trained on our simulation data achieves higher success rates on most tasks than that trained on real-robot teleoperation data, owing largely to the broad lighting and environmental variations covered by our simulation rendering, which real-robot data fails to capture. The project page is available at https://oasis-humanoid.github.io/.

</details>


### [260] [FAWAM: Force-Aware World Action Models for Closed-Loop Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08555)
*Haotian He,Zeyu Yan,Qipeng Liu,Ning Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAWAM，一种将力信号融入感知、预测和闭环执行三个层次的力感知世界动作模型，用于接触丰富的机器人操作任务。FAWAM编码历史六轴力/力矩信号来调制动作生成，联合预测未来动作和末端执行器力/力矩以显式建模接触演化，并引入残差修正模块利用预测的力/力矩轨迹作为执行时参考，基于实时力反馈在线优化动作。真实实验表明，FAWAM相比纯视觉基线提升成功率36.25%，相比现有力感知基线提升21.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多将力信号仅作为额外观测模态，未充分利用其建模未来交互动力学或引导执行时反馈修正的潜力。

Method: 在三个层面融入力信息：(1) 感知层：编码历史六轴力/力矩信号调制动作生成；(2) 预测层：联合预测未来动作和末端执行器力/力矩，显式建模接触演化；(3) 闭环执行层：引入残差修正模块，以预测的力/力矩轨迹为参考，基于实时力反馈在线修正动作。

Result: 在多个接触丰富的真实机器人任务中，FAWAM相比纯视觉基线平均成功率提升36.25%，相比现有力感知基线提升21.25%。

Conclusion: 力感知框架对于鲁棒的接触丰富操作任务非常有效，在三个层面系统性地融入力信息能显著提升操作成功率。

Abstract: Force signals provide critical interaction cues for contact-rich robotic manipulation. However, existing methods mostly use force as an additional observation modality, without fully exploiting its role in modeling future interaction dynamics or guiding execution-time feedback correction. In this paper, we propose FAWAM, a force-aware world action model that incorporates force information at three levels: perception, prediction, and closed-loop execution. FAWAM first encodes historical 6-axis force/torque signals to modulate action generation, then jointly predicts future actions and end-effector wrenches to explicitly model contact evolution. It further introduces a residual correction module that uses the predicted wrench trajectory as an execution-time reference to refine actions online based on real-time force feedback. Real-world experiments across multiple contact-rich tasks show that FAWAM improves the average success rate by 36.25% over vision-only baselines and 21.25% over existing force-aware baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of our force-aware framework for robust contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [261] [Real-IKEA: Physical Fidelity is the Prerequisite for Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08564)
*Kunqi Xu,Zhenhao Huang,Siyuan Luo,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Real-IKEA是一个面向物理精确性的铰接物体操作数据集和仿真框架，包含1079个来自83种真实IKEA把手/旋钮的资产配置，通过双向表面偏差度量和阻力校准动力学，证明高保真资产能通过RL学习出利用机械优势的鲁棒操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作的鲁棒性常因简化仿真与真实世界之间的物理差距而失败，现有工作缺乏对铰接交互中物理真实性的重视，需要高保真数据集和仿真框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 构建包含1079种铰接资产配置的数据集，来自83个真实IKEA把手/旋钮，经过六步物理工作流处理；2) 提出双向表面偏差度量（bidirectional surface-deviation metric）用于量化碰撞网格的接触几何精度；3) 建立阻力校准配置（resistance-calibrated configurations），改变阻尼和摩擦力以实现动力学真实感；4) 使用RL策略验证高保真资产的有效性。

Result: 高保真资产使RL策略能够发现鲁棒的"钩拉"（hooking）和"杠杆"（levering）策略，这些策略优先利用机械优势而非脆弱的摩擦拉动，实现了类人水平的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Real-IKEA作为铰接物体操作任务的关键基准，证明了物理精确性对学习鲁棒操作策略的重要性，为开发具备人类级别鲁棒性的操作策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation robustness often founders on the physics gap between simplified simulations and the resistance-laden real world. In this work, we emphasize that physical realism in articulated interaction is an important ingredient for robust policy learning. We present Real-IKEA, a dataset and simulation framework designed with physical accuracy as a first-class goal. Real-IKEA provides 1,079 articulated asset configurations, derived from 83 authentic IKEA handles and knobs processed through a meticulous six-step physical workflow. For contact-geometry accuracy, we introduce a bidirectional surface-deviation metric to quantify collision meshes. For dynamics realism, we establish resistance-calibrated configurations that vary damping and friction. Crucially, we demonstrate through a Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy that high-fidelity assets enable the discovery of robust "hooking" and "levering" strategies that prioritize mechanical advantage over fragile friction-pulling. Together, these results position Real-IKEA as a critical benchmark for developing manipulation policies capable of human-level robustness in articulated object tasks.

</details>


### [262] [HARBOR: A Harness Framework for Agentic Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08610)
*Zechu Li,Yufeng Jin,Xiaoyang Liu,Puze Liu,Vignesh Prasad,Carlo D'Eramo,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HARBOR是一个智能体框架，将机器人强化学习的sim-to-real工作流自动化，包括环境搭建、奖励设计、超参数调优等，通过标准化命令、持久化工件、可执行门控和可复用知识，将高层目标分解为多阶段任务并由专用智能体执行，支持分布式并行试错与跨运行经验学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人RL的工程管线（任务构建、奖励设计、超参数调优）依赖大量专家手动操作，成本高、难扩展，限制了RL的广泛采用，因此需要自动化框架降低工程门槛。

Method: 将自动化问题建模为"harness-engineering"问题，智能体通过标准化命令、持久化工件、可执行门控和可复用知识，将高层目标分解为有界的阶段任务，由专用智能体逐步执行，同时通过分布式并行试错和跨运行经验学习实现规模化迭代。

Result: 在6个基准、16个任务（涵盖操作、 locomotion、双臂灵巧控制）上验证，HARBOR能端到端自动化仿真RL工作流，设计奖励、调优算法达到或超过默认配置，降低工程成本，且训练策略可迁移到真实机器人。

Conclusion: HARBOR证明了利用智能体自动化机器人RL工程管线的可行性，能显著降低人工成本同时保持甚至提升策略性能，且sim-to-real可部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful paradigm for robot learning, particularly in sim-to-real settings, but its broader adoption remains limited by the engineering pipeline surrounding the algorithms. Building tasks, shaping rewards, and tuning hyperparameters require substantial expert effort, making RL workflows costly and difficult to scale. We introduce HARBOR, an agentic framework that frames robot RL automation as a harness-engineering problem: given a simulator codebase and a task specification, it automates the workflow from environment setup to policy training in simulation. HARBOR decomposes such high-level objectives into bounded stages executed by specialized agents through standardized commands, persistent artifacts, executable gates, and reusable knowledge, and scales iteration via decentralized parallel trials and experience learning across runs. We evaluate HARBOR across 6 benchmarks and 16 tasks in total, spanning manipulation, locomotion, and bimanual dexterous control. We demonstrate that HARBOR automates the simulation RL workflow end-to-end, designs rewards, tunes algorithms to match or improve over default configurations, and reduces engineering effort at practical token and wall-clock cost; the resulting policies can also be transferred to real robots.

</details>


### [263] [PhysGraph: A Physics-aware 3D Scene Graph for Perception and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08655)
*Haoyu Li,Aaron Thomas,Shuyan Zhou,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysGraph是一个统一符号推理与结构化3D几何的框架，能从RGB-D观测中重建面向物体的3D几何，将物体分解为功能部件并推断材质与关节属性，生成语义丰富且物理一致的场景图，支持多物体质量估计、关节预测、约束感知的3D affordance预测及real-to-sim迁移等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景表示方法侧重语义检索，缺乏对物理和运动学因素的建模；而尝试建模物理属性的方法受限于窄训练集或单物体建模，难以泛化到多样物体类型。需要一个统一框架来建模杂乱场景中的运动学与物理属性。

Method: 从RGB-D观测出发，重建物体级3D几何并跨视图关联实例；然后将物体分解为功能部件，通过视觉推理推断材质与关节类型；最终生成物理一致且语义结构化的场景图。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，PhysGraph在语义分割、多物体质量估计和关节预测任务上达到SOTA结果，并成功应用于约束感知的3D affordance预测和real-to-sim迁移。

Conclusion: PhysGraph提供了一种简单有效的结构化3D表示方案，统一了物理属性建模与语义推理，为下游机器人任务提供了物理一致且语义丰富的场景理解基础。

Abstract: To perform a wide range of daily tasks, robots need to construct a 3D representation that is semantically rich, physically grounded, and structured enough to support task planning and affordance prediction. However, existing approaches primarily focus on semantic retrieval, often overlooking physical and kinematic factors. Methods that attempt to model physical properties typically rely on narrow training sets or single-object modeling, limiting scalability and generalization across diverse object types. To address these challenges, we present PhysGraph, a framework that unifies symbolic reasoning with structured 3D geometry to model kinematic and physical properties in cluttered scenes. Given RGB-D observations, PhysGraph reconstructs object-centric 3D geometry and associates object instances across views. It then decomposes objects into functional parts and infers materials and articulations through visual reasoning. Evaluated on both synthetic and real-world datasets, PhysGraph achieves state-of-the-art results in semantic segmentation, multi-object mass estimation, and articulation prediction. With its simple yet effective design, PhysGraph produces physically consistent and semantically structured scene graphs, serving as a structured 3D representation for downstream tasks such as constraint-aware 3D affordance prediction and real-to-sim transfer, both of which are demonstrated in our experiments.

</details>


### [264] [Latent Diffusion Policy: Shaping Latent Spaces for Diffusion-Based Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08657)
*Zhexuan Zhou,Yichen Lai,Jinhao Zhang,Huizhe Li,Youmin Gong,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Latent Diffusion Policy (LDP)，一种两阶段框架，通过CVAE编码器吸收场景理解到潜空间，再在预浓缩的潜空间中进行流匹配生成动作轨迹，从而解耦场景理解和轨迹生成，在协调密集型任务上显著优于DP3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的视觉运动策略直接在原始动作空间中操作，场景理解与轨迹生成耦合在单一去噪过程中，导致速度场需同时编码场景信息和生成精确轨迹，增加了学习复杂度，限制了需要多臂精确时间协调的任务的性能。

Method: 1) 两阶段框架：CVAE编码器吸收场景信息将观测条件分布浓缩到潜空间；2) 在潜空间中用流匹配(flow matching)生成，速度场更平滑；3) 逐token扩散训练强制和阶梯式推理采样解决分布不匹配；4) 提出reconstruction FID (rFID)作为轻量级代理评估指标。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的协调密集型任务上显著优于DP3，并成功迁移到真实双臂部署场景。

Conclusion: 通过将场景理解解耦到CVAE中，潜空间扩散策略简化了学习问题，在需要精确时间协调的多臂任务上表现优异，且能有效迁移到真实机器人系统。

Abstract: Diffusion-based visuomotor policies operating directly in raw action spaces conflate scene comprehension with trajectory generation within a single denoising process. The resulting velocity field must simultaneously encode scene information and generate precise trajectories, increasing learning complexity and limiting performance on tasks demanding precise temporal coordination across multiple arms. To simplify this joint learning problem, we introduce Latent Diffusion Policy (LDP), a two-stage framework performing flow matching in a deliberately shaped latent space. By absorbing scene understanding into an observation-conditioned CVAE encoder, LDP concentrates the conditional distribution of each observation. Consequently, the flow model avoids implicitly resolving scene-dependent structures; instead, it generates within a pre-concentrated distribution featuring a smoother velocity field, simplifying learning from limited demonstrations. Furthermore, to capture temporal dependencies among latent tokens, LDP trains with per-token diffusion forcing and employs staircase inference sampling to resolve the resulting distributional mismatch. We also propose reconstruction FID (rFID) as a lightweight proxy predicting downstream task success solely from latent space statistics. On coordination-intensive tasks from RoboTwin 2.0, LDP outperforms DP3 by a substantial margin and transfers effectively to real-world bimanual deployments.

</details>


### [265] [Language as a Sensor: Calibrated Spatial Belief Estimation in 3D Scenes from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08666)
*Aryan Naveen,Jason Xinyu Liu,Luca Carlone,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语言传感器模型(LSM)将自然语言描述转化为校准的空间分布，并构建VL-Map概率框架将其与视觉感知融合，在VLA-3D基准和真实机器人上实现更精确的目标定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类环境中常接收自然语言空间信息，但这些信息所指场景往往超出其感知视场。传统度量语义建图忽略语言信号，现有多模态模型在3D空间推理上有限且难以与其他传感器融合。

Method: 1) 训练Language Sensor Model(LSM)，将每条语言描述及其场景图上下文映射为多模态分布，混合权重编码指代歧义，分量协方差编码空间不确定性；2) 提出VL-Map概率框架，将语言预测作为随机观测与机载感知在统一置信图中融合。

Result: 在VLA-3D基准和真实机器人上，LSM是唯一协方差估计保持在校准范围内的语言预测器；VL-Map融合后相比最强基础模型基线，目标物体位置预测概率质量提高约70%。

Conclusion: LSM能有效将语言观测转化为校准的空间分布，VL-Map框架成功融合语言与视觉感知，显著提升非视场目标定位能力。

Abstract: Robots deployed in human-centric environments routinely receive natural-language descriptions of spatial information ("I left my backpack on the table") that reference parts of the world beyond their perceptual field of view. Traditional metric-semantic mapping ignores this signal, while off-the-shelf multimodal models remain limited in 3D spatial reasoning and are not directly amenable to fusion with other sensor modalities. To convert language observations into a calibrated spatial distribution, we train a Language Sensor Model (LSM) that maps each utterance and its scene-graph context to a multimodal distribution, with mixture weights encoding referential ambiguity (e.g., "which table") and component covariances encoding spatial uncertainty (e.g., where "on the table" the target lies). We then introduce VL-Map (Vision-Language Metric-Semantic Mapping), a probabilistic framework that treats these language predictions as stochastic observations and fuses them with onboard perception within a unified belief map. On the VLA-3D benchmark as well as on a real-world mobile robot, LSM is the only language predictor whose covariance estimates remain within the calibrated regime; fused into VL-Map, it leads to more accurate predictions of the target object location (~70% more probability mass on the true target compared to the strongest foundation-model baseline).

</details>


### [266] [PhysAgent: Automating Physics-Based 4D Synthesis via Trajectory-Grounded Multi-Agent Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08688)
*Chunji Lv,Jiaxi Ye,Yuchen Jiang,Rexar Lin,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysAgent 是首个将仿真器置于循环中的多智能体框架，通过语义智能体初始化力场并用细化智能体基于多智能体轨迹反馈进行零样本宏观跳跃，实现从多模态输入自动生成物理合理的4D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在自动配置复杂环境力场时存在严重瓶颈：手动专家干预成本高，LLM缺乏仿真反馈导致物理不准确，SDS梯度缓慢且无法动态切换离散力场。

Method: 解耦内禀材料与外禀动力学，语义智能体（含外力场技能模块）生成有效初始化，细化智能体通过轨迹接地多智能体反馈（视觉基础模型提取密集点轨迹→转为文本描述→LLM常识推理）执行零样本跳跃，逃离局部最优并动态切换离散力场。

Result: PhysAgent能从任意多模态提示快速生成稳定、多样的物理场景，在生成多样性和物理准确性上显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 首次实现仿真器在环的多智能体自动化物理4D合成，有效解决了力场优化中的模态差距和技术缺陷。

Abstract: Achieving fully automated, physically plausible 3D motion synthesis is a core objective in graphics and generative AI. However, configuring complex environmental force fields still relies entirely on manual expert intervention, creating a severe bottleneck for large-scale simulation data generation. Existing automated methods primarily focus on material optimization and exhibit severe modality gaps and technical flaws when applied to the vastly more complex force field optimization space: naive Large Language Models (LLMs) lack underlying simulation feedback, causing severe physical inaccuracies, while traditional Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) suffers from sluggish gradients, local optima entrapment, and a mathematical inability to dynamically switch discrete force fields. To address this, we propose PhysAgent, the first simulator-in-the-loop multi-agent framework that leverages multimodal inputs for automated, physically grounded 4D synthesis. By decoupling intrinsic materials from extrinsic dynamics, PhysAgent utilizes a Semantic Agent equipped with an externalized Force Field Skill module to master simulation rules and generate valid initializations. Subsequently, the Refine Agents, driven by Trajectory-Grounded Multi-Agent Feedback, leverage vision foundation models to extract dense point trajectories from rendered frames. By converting these explicit motion trajectories into structured textual descriptors, the agent harnesses LLM commonsense reasoning to execute zero-shot macroscopic leaps, effectively escaping local optima and dynamically switching discrete force fields. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PhysAgent rapidly generates stable, diverse physical scenes from arbitrary multimodal prompts, significantly outperforming existing baselines in both generation diversity and physical accuracy.

</details>


### [267] [Real-Time and Accurate Collision-Free Teleoperation via Differentiable Constraint-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08725)
*Max Grobbel,Tristan Schneider,Daniel Flögel,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对遥操作中仅控制末端位姿导致的自碰撞与环境碰撞问题，本文利用基于凸优化对偶性的可微碰撞避免约束，将机器人建模为胶囊体、环境建模为多面体，实现了计算更快、建模更精确的轨迹规划方法，在仿真和真实UR5e机械臂上验证了平滑且无碰撞的遥操作效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作中仅控制末端位姿易导致机械臂自碰撞和环境碰撞，现有基于最优控制的轨迹规划方法要么用球体近似损失几何精度，要么数值近似导数降低收敛性和增加计算时间，亟需一种既保持几何精度又高效可微的碰撞约束方法。

Method: 采用基于凸优化对偶性的可微碰撞避免约束公式，将机器人建模为胶囊体（capsules）、环境建模为多面体（polytopes），并适配至遥操作场景的轨迹规划中。

Result: 在仿真中与当前最优方法对比（不同障碍物数量），并在真实UR5e机械臂上进行遥操作测试，结果表明本方法计算时间更低，障碍物建模更精确，实现了更平滑且无碰撞的末端遥操作。

Conclusion: 基于对偶性可微碰撞约束的遥操作轨迹规划方法在计算效率和几何精度上均优于现有方法，能够有效解决遥操作中的碰撞问题。

Abstract: In teleoperation, the human operator typically controls only the end-effector pose, which often leads to self-collisions of the manipulator and collisions with environmental obstacles, since joints and links are not controlled individually. A common strategy to mitigate this issue is to enhance the operator's input using optimal-control-based trajectory planning. As derivative-based solvers require differentiable constraints, existing approaches either approximate robots and obstacles with spheres, reducing geometric accuracy, or approximate derivatives, degrading convergence and increasing computation times. We address these limitations by adapting a recent formulation of differentiable collision-avoidance constraints, based on duality in convex optimization, to the teleoperation setting. The robot is approximated with capsules and the environment with polytopes. We compare the resulting trajectory planning method against state-of-the-art techniques in simulation with varying numbers of obstacles and evaluate it on a UR5e manipulator in a real-world teleoperation test. Results show that our approach achieves lower computation times while enabling more accurate obstacle modeling, leading to smoother and collision-free end-effector teleoperation.

</details>


### [268] [IR-SIM: A Lightweight Skill-Native Simulator for Navigation, Learning, and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08729)
*Ruihua Han,Shuai Wang,Chengyang Li,Rui Gao,Xinyi Wang,Zhe Liu,Guoliang Li,Yupu Lu,Qi Hao,Jia Pan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IR-SIM轻量级仿真器，通过YAML配置完全定义机器人导航场景，支持从自然语言生成/修改场景，用于快速原型设计、基准测试和机器人学习，并可桥接高保真仿真器和真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真器需要自定义代码或复杂接口，阻碍了基于LLM的快速原型开发和自动化算法研究，因此需要一个轻量、可描述、可复现的导航仿真器。

Method: 场景完全由YAML配置文件定义（运动学、碰撞检测、LiDAR、可视化、行为模块）；通过IR-SIM agent skills实现从文本提示自动生成/修改YAML配置；提供到高保真仿真器和真实部署的桥接。

Result: 实验展示了：从自然语言构建导航场景、训练碰撞避免策略、社交导航策略基准测试、以及桥接到高保真仿真器和真实部署等多个任务的便利性和通用性。

Conclusion: IR-SIM作为轻量级技能原生导航仿真器，通过YAML配置实现了仿真场景的完全可描述和可复现，有效降低了机器人仿真原型开发的门槛。

Abstract: Simulation plays a key role in automated robotics research supported by large language models (LLMs). However, existing simulators often require custom code or complex interfaces, creating a barrier to rapid prototyping and automated algorithm development. To this end, we propose the Intelligent Robot Simulator (IR-SIM), a lightweight skill-native navigation simulator designed for rapid scenario construction, benchmarking, and robot learning. In IR-SIM, scenarios are entirely defined by YAML configuration files that specify mobile robot kinematics, geometric collision checking, LiDAR sensing, visualization, and behavior modules. This design makes robotic simulation fully describable and reproducible, allowing scenarios to be generated and modified from text prompts through the proposed IR-SIM agent skills. The resulting scenarios can be used for automated benchmarking of navigation algorithms and for automated generation of training data for learning methods. Furthermore, IR-SIM provides bridges to high fidelity simulators and real world deployment, allowing users to validate their algorithms in more realistic settings after prototyping without extra coding. The experiments showcase the convenience and versatility of IR-SIM in multiple tasks: constructing navigation scenarios from natural language, training a collision avoidance policy, benchmarking social navigation policies, and bridging to high fidelity simulators and real world deployment. The project website is available at https://github.com/hanruihua/ir-sim.

</details>


### [269] [Dream-Tac: A Unified Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08737)
*Yunfan Lou,Yifan Ye,Yankai Fu,Jun Cen,Xiaowei Chi,Yaoxu Lyu,Peidong Jia,Sirui Han,Zhihe Lu,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream-Tac 提出统一触觉-视觉世界动作模型，通过接触门控融合和接触感知注意力偏置，结合双重加速策略，在接触丰富的操作任务中将动作精度平均提升31.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型主要依赖视觉，在接触丰富的操作任务中缺乏触觉信号，无法捕捉物理交互中的关键线索，导致操作失败。

Method: (i) 接触门控视觉-触觉融合（contact-gated visuotactile fusion）选择性整合触觉信号；(ii) 接触感知注意力偏置（contact-aware attention bias）调节跨模态交互；(iii) 双重加速策略：训练阶段重构注意力路径保持融合，推理阶段采用缓存扩散加速。

Result: 在六项接触丰富操作任务上，动作精度平均提升31.7%；训练速度提升2.9倍，推理速度提升1.8倍。

Conclusion: 统一视觉-触觉世界建模能有效提升接触丰富操作任务的动作生成质量，且具备实时部署潜力。

Abstract: World action models inherit the predictive capability of world models, enabling action generation to be guided by anticipated future observations. However, they rely primarily on vision and often fail in contact-rich manipulation, where critical cues arise from physical interaction. In this paper, we propose Dream-Tac, a unified Tactile-World Action Model that jointly models actions, future visual observations, and tactile dynamics. Specifically, Dream-Tac introduces (i) contact-gated visuotactile fusion to selectively integrate tactile signals and (ii) a contact-aware attention bias to better regulate cross-modal interactions during manipulation. To support real-time deployment, we further design a dual-level acceleration strategy, reformulating the contact-aware bias to preserve the fused attention path during training and introducing cache-based diffusion acceleration at inference, achieving up to 2.9$\times$ faster training and 1.8$\times$ faster inference. Across six contact-rich manipulation tasks, Dream-Tac improves action accuracy by 31.7\% on average, demonstrating the effectiveness of unified visuotactile world modeling.Code is available at https://github.com/LYFCLOUDFAN/Dream-Tac.

</details>


### [270] [Safe, Fluent and Acceptable Motion Generation and Execution for Human--Robot Interaction in Manufacturing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08741)
*Thibaut Lopez,Olivier Aycard,Pierre-Brice Wieber,Mohamed Boua,Christine Jeoffrion*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在人机共享环境中，机器人运动生成策略如何兼顾安全性与社会可接受性（如运动平滑度、人类舒适度）。通过识别影响人类感知的运动参数，基于MPC框架生成四种社会感知机器人行为，并通过用户研究验证了不同行为对社会可接受性的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人运动控制主要关注物理安全，但人机共享环境中机器人还需具备社会可接受性、流畅性和舒适性等心理认知与社会维度特性，现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 1) 识别影响人类感知的运动参数；2) 基于Model Predictive Control (MPC)框架生成四种不同的社会感知机器人行为；3) 开展用户研究，评估非专业参与者对机器人行为的社会感知。

Result: 机器人行为的变异显著影响系统的社会可接受性，验证了将人类中心考量融入运动生成策略的重要性。

Conclusion: 人机共享环境中的机器人运动不能仅考虑安全，必须从社会与心理认知维度设计行为，以提升交互的流畅性和可接受性。

Abstract: Robots operating in human environments must not only ensure physical safety but also exhibit behaviors that are understandable, fluent, and acceptable to human partners. This paper investigates motion generation strategies that combine safety guarantees with interaction quality considerations, such as motion smoothness and human comfort. While the design of robots capable of ensuring safety in shared human-robot environments has enabled closer and more advanced forms of interaction, these new proximity-based tasks require moving beyond purely technical considerations. In particular, robot behavior must also be addressed from psycho-cognitive and social perspectives. In this context, we argue for the relevance of integrating social-aware motion control into robotic systems. First, we identify the motion parameters that influence human perception and operator experience. Then, we implement a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that generates four distinct socially-informed robot behaviors. Finally, we conduct a user study to evaluate and validate these behaviors and assess their social impact on non-expert participants. The results demonstrate that variations in robot behavior significantly affect the perceived social acceptability of the system. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating human-centered considerations into motion generation strategies for robots operating in shared environments.

</details>


### [271] [Guided Discovery of New Behaviors using Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08743)
*Dian Yu,Sebastian Sanokowski,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合Feynman-Kac修正器与引导势能的框架，用于从少量示教中系统性地挖掘和修复扩散策略中的稀缺但有效的行为模式，通过采样轨迹优化和重训练实现多样化行为发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在处理多模态动作-轨迹分布时表现优异，但在演示数据有限的情况下，标准采样倾向于复制主导行为而忽略有效但罕见的模式，现有指导方法或强化学习组合要么将样本推向不可行区域，要么难以跳出局部最优，无法系统性地发现多样化行为。

Method: 将Feynman-Kac修正器与新的引导势能结合，引导扩散策略样本向有前景但代表性不足的轨迹偏移；通过基于采样的轨迹优化精炼这些轨迹；将精炼后的轨迹重新纳入训练集以重训扩散策略。

Result: 在多种操作环境中验证了该框架的有效性，能一致地发现新行为，证明其能系统性地挖掘和修复新颖轨迹，生成多样化且可执行的行为。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了少样本场景下扩散策略行为多样性不足的问题，通过组合Feynman-Kac修正、引导势能和轨迹优化，实现了从有限演示中系统性地发现多样化、可行的机器人行为。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a powerful tool for generative modeling in robotics, with diffusion policies excelling at modeling multimodal action-trajectory distributions. However, when demonstrations are limited, standard sampling often reproduces dominant behaviors while neglecting valid but rare modes, limiting the discovery of novel solutions. Existing approaches, such as guidance methods or combining reinforcement learning with diffusion, either push samples into infeasible regions or struggle to escape local minima, failing to systematically uncover diverse behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose a framework that combines Feynman-Kac correctors with a novel guiding potential that systematically guides diffusion policy samples towards promising yet underrepresented samples. These trajectories are refined using sampling-based trajectory optimization and reincorporated into the training set to retrain the diffusion policy. Our method effectively mines and repairs novel trajectories, enabling the systematic discovery of diverse and executable behaviors. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework across a range of manipulation environments, consistently discovering new behaviors.

</details>


### [272] [RGB-S: Image-Aligned Tactile Saliency for Robust Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08765)
*Shengcheng Luo,Kefei Wu,Xiaoying Zhou,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将触觉信号显式投影到RGB图像平面并渲染力调制高斯显著性图的方法，通过零初始化条件架构将物理接触先验注入视觉骨干网络，显著提升严重遮挡下的灵巧操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式跨模态对应学习，数据效率低且在视觉退化时泛化差，缺乏几何先验来鲁棒地对齐稀疏异构触觉测量与密集视觉表征。

Method: 利用正运动学和相机标定将触觉传感器位置投影到RGB图像平面，渲染力调制的高斯显著性图以建模空间不确定性，通过零初始化条件架构将2D空间锚点注入标准视觉骨干，保持预训练视觉表征不变。

Result: 在严重视觉遮挡下，真实世界实验显示显式RGB-触觉接地相比最强的隐式基线在成功率高26.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式的视觉-触觉空间接地能有效提升遮挡下的空间推理能力和操作鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective visuo-tactile integration is critical for robotic dexterous manipulation, especially when visual observations are unreliable or occluded. However, robustly aligning sparse, heterogeneous tactile measurements with dense visual representations remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing approaches require policies to learn cross-modal correspondences implicitly from limited demonstrations, without leveraging geometric priors. As a result, they are often data-inefficient and generalize poorly when visual observations are degraded. To address this limitation, we propose a framework that explicitly grounds physical contacts in the image domain. Using robot forward kinematics and camera calibration, we project tactile sensor locations directly onto the RGB image plane. We then render force-modulated Gaussian saliency maps to model spatial uncertainty arising from kinematic and calibration errors. By integrating these 2D spatial anchors through a zero-initialized conditioning architecture, our method injects physical contact priors into standard visual backbones while preserving pre-trained visual representations. We evaluate our method on six dexterous manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world under severe visual occlusions. Real-world experiments show that explicit RGB-S grounding in the image domain improves real-world occluded manipulation success rates by $26.7$ percentage points over the strongest implicit visuo-tactile baseline, suggesting its improved spatial reasoning and robustness to occlusion. Project page: touch-as-saliency.github.io

</details>


### [273] [Unifying Object-Centric World Models and Diffusion Policy: A Hierarchical Framework for Multi-Stage Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08775)
*Raktim Gautam Goswami,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Yann LeCun,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldDP是一个分层世界模型框架，通过高层世界模型优化可行子目标、低层扩散策略执行，结合对象中心表示，实现多阶段机器人操作任务，性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型在机器人领域仅能处理单阶段任务（如抓取），无法应对需要复杂序列规划的多阶段操作任务。

Method: 提出分层框架WorldDP：高层世界模型作为转移函数，在运行时优化可行子目标；低层扩散策略执行子目标；引入对象中心表示解耦环境实体，支持按实体顺序规划。

Result: 在多个机器人操作基准上，WorldDP一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 将世界模型的物理基础规划与扩散策略的高效执行相结合，能够显著提升多阶段机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Visual world models have shown great potential in learning complex system dynamics. Recent advancements leverage these models as transition functions within Model Predictive Control (MPC) frameworks to solve various control tasks. When applied to robotics, however, they are limited to single-stage tasks such as reaching or grasping, and struggle with multi-stage ones that demand complex sequential planning. In this work, we introduce WorldDP, a world model framework designed for multi-stage robotic manipulation. Our hierarchical approach utilizes a high-level world model as a transition function to optimize for feasible subgoals during runtime, which are subsequently reached by a low-level Diffusion Policy. To further aid in learning dynamics and planning, we incorporate object-centric representations that decouple environmental entities and enable us to plan sequentially with respect to each. Evaluated across several robotics benchmarks, WorldDP consistently outperforms existing baselines, validating that coupling the world model's physically grounded planning with diffusion policy's efficient execution yields superior multi-stage performance.

</details>


### [274] [Video2Sim2Real: Full-Stack Autonomous Dexterous Skill Acquisition from a Single Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08828)
*Yunhai Han,Jianuo Qiu,Linhao Bai,Ziyu Xiao,Zihang Zeng,Yangcen Liu,Zhaodong Yang,Shalin Jain,Wenrui Ma,Jiaqi Fu,Yuqian Zheng,Manisha Natarajan,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Kenneth Shaw,Matthew Gombolay,Zsolt Kira,Harish Ravichandar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Video2Sim2Real全栈框架，从单个人类操作视频中学习灵巧技能，通过对象中心关键帧+仿真优化+残差强化学习+碰撞感知规划，实现从视频到仿真再到真实机器人的技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类操作视频是便捷的机器人学习来源，但感知误差和具身差距使得直接迁移灵巧操作到机器人上仍然困难。

Method: 1) 利用基础模型从视频重建仿真数字双胞胎并提取运动先验；2) 提取对象中心关键帧，在仿真中优化对应机器人构型作为锚点；3) Sim-to-Real：用IL从噪声点云重校准构型+残差RL进行局部手指级适应；4) 碰撞感知运动规划实现空间泛化。

Result: 在多个日常操作任务中，Video2Sim2Real在仿真中提升了任务成功率、安全性和轨迹一致性，且Sim-to-Real迁移优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 证明了从人类视频自主获取灵巧技能是一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Human manipulation videos are a convenient and intuitive source for robot learning. However, directly transferring human dexterity to robots remains challenging due to perception errors and embodiment gap. To address this, we introduce Video2Sim2Real, a full-stack framework for autonomous skill acquisition from a single human manipulation video. Our framework first uses off-the-shelf foundation models to reconstruct a simulator-ready digital twin and extract robot and object motion priors. Rather than treating the extracted robot motion as a reliable reference throughout execution, our key idea is to recover and leverage the most fundamental sources of supervision from the demonstrated skill: We identify object-centric keyframes to optimize the corresponding robot configurations using object information from the simulator, and use these configurations as anchors that refine the robot motion such that it ultimately has the desired impact on the environment. To bridge the remaining sim-to-real gap, we introduce a sim-to-real strategy that decouples robustness to noisy and incomplete perception from variations in hand-object interaction dynamics. Specifically, we learn to recalibrate robot configurations from noisy real-world point clouds via IL, and leverage residual RL to perform local finger-level adaptations to ensure for robust and effective interactions. Finally, a collision-aware motion planning module enables spatial generalization to novel object configurations. Across several everyday manipulation tasks, Video2Sim2Real improves simulated task success, safety, and trajectory coherence over numerous baselines, and achieves better sim-to-real transfer than existing techniques. These results demonstrate a promising path toward autonomous dexterous skill acquisition from human videos.

</details>


### [275] [Benchmarking Vision-Language-Action Models on SO-101: Failure and Recovery Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08881)
*Yi Yu,Xinchuan Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SO-101基准，在低成本真实机器人平台上系统评估VLA和模仿学习策略（π₀.₅、SmolVLA、Wall-X、ACT），除任务成功率外还引入故障分类、失败分解和恢复能力指标，发现执行不稳定是主要失败原因，恢复能力因架构而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估主要在仿真或昂贵机器人平台上进行，缺乏在低成本真实机器人上的鲁棒性系统评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在SO-101低成本平台上设计四项代表性操作任务及统一评估协议，对π₀.₅、SmolVLA、Wall-X和ACT四种策略进行真实遥操作数据微调和评估，引入结构化故障分类、语义/执行级失败分解和恢复感知评估指标。

Result: 预训练VLA策略整体优于模仿学习基线，但性能高度依赖任务；执行不稳定是主要失败来源；不同架构的恢复能力差异显著。

Conclusion: 超越二元任务成功率的失败与恢复分析至关重要，SO-101可作为低成本真实机器人条件下评估具身AI系统的实用基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet existing evaluations are primarily conducted in simulation or on expensive robotic platforms, leaving their robustness on affordable real-world robots largely unexplored. We present a standardized real-world benchmark for evaluating representative VLA and imitation learning policies on the low-cost SO-101 robotic platform. The benchmark comprises four representative manipulation tasks together with unified evaluation protocols, enabling systematic comparison under embodiment uncertainty. Using real-world teleoperated demonstrations, we fine-tune and evaluate $π_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, Wall-X, and ACT directly on the physical platform. Beyond conventional task success rates, the benchmark incorporates a structured failure taxonomy, semantic- and execution-level failure decomposition, and recovery-aware evaluation metrics to characterize policy robustness. Experimental results show that stronger pretrained VLA policies generally outperform the imitation learning baseline, although performance remains highly task-dependent under low-cost robotic deployment conditions. Execution instability emerges as the dominant failure source, while recovery capability varies substantially across architectures. These results highlight the importance of failure and recovery analysis beyond binary task success and establish SO-101 as a practical benchmark for evaluating embodied AI systems under realistic low-cost robotic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [276] [PTDL:Multi-Terrain Fall Recovery via Phase-Terrain Decoupled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08922)
*Xiaoyu Xu,Zhiming Chen,Yuenan Zhao,Ran Song,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PTDL方法，通过解耦阶段和地形轴训练，让单一人形机器人策略在摔倒后能在不同地形（平地、砾石、斜坡）上自主恢复并继续行走，仅依赖本体感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在混合地形上训练时，由于未分离恢复与行走阶段、未考虑逐地形约束，导致策略退化为单一妥协起身动作，无法在不同地形上实现差异化恢复。

Method: PTDL：阶段轴上使用投影重力门控的双运动先验判别器+探地到行走过渡；地形轴上使用地形分层恢复塑形，为不同地形分配特定训练信号，地形标签仅训练时使用，部署时策略隐式选择。

Result: 在Unitree G1上验证，支持平地、砾石、20度斜坡，实现稳定跨地形恢复、平滑过渡和差异化起身行为，仅用单一策略。

Conclusion: PTDL有效解决了人形机器人多地形摔倒恢复与连续行走的难题，通过解耦训练实现了单一策略下的多地形自适应恢复。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can fall on slopes, gravel, and uneven ground in unstructured environments. We target integrated fall recovery and locomotion: rebuilding balance from a fallen state using proprioception alone and resuming velocity-commanded walking at the fall site. Prior methods often stop at quasi-static rise, neglect the post-fall ground-contact phase, or, when trained on mixed terrains without separating recovery and locomotion phases or per-surface constraints, collapse to a single compromise get-up across surfaces. We propose Phase--Terrain Decoupled Learning (PTDL), which decouples training supervision along phase and terrain axes while deploying one proprioceptive policy. On the phase axis, projected-gravity-gated dual motion-prior discriminators and a probe-to-walk transition link post-fall recovery to commanded walking. On the terrain axis, terrain-stratified recovery shaping assigns surface-specific training supervision on flat ground, gravel, and slopes; terrain labels are training-only and withheld from policy observations, enabling implicit post-fall strategy selection at deployment. We validate PTDL on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 across flat ground, gravel, and slopes up to 20 degrees in simulation and on hardware, achieving stable cross-terrain recovery, smooth recovery-to-locomotion transitions, and differentiated post-fall rise behaviors under one deployed policy.

</details>


### [277] [SpaceVLN: A Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation Agent with Online Spatial Cognitive Memory and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08992)
*Yucheng Deng,Pingrui Lai,Xinhai Li,Chenjia Bai,Xiaoheng Deng,Chengnuo Sun,Xuelong Li,Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpaceVLN通过空间认知记忆和任务引导空间推理，在零样本设置下统一了连续环境中的视觉-语言导航和物体目标导航，无需任务特定策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航智能体依赖局部视觉线索和线性历史推理，忽视了导航中空间区域、路径、地标及其空间关系的本质，缺乏对探索区域的空间结构化理解。

Method: 提出SpaceVLN，包含：1) 空间认知记忆：将探索区域抽象为空间路点并动态维护子任务地标证据，形成分层记忆；2) Spatial-CoT：结合任务进度推理与空间感知、分析、预测，实现任务引导空间推理；3) 阶段化闭环框架，统一处理VLN和Object-Goal导航。

Result: 在R2R-CE、RxR-CE、GN-Bench、HM3D-OVON上取得零样本SOTA性能，并在真实机器人上验证了部署可行性。

Conclusion: 空间认知记忆和任务引导空间推理是构建更强具身导航智能体的实用基础，且能在零样本统一框架下处理多种导航任务。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in continuous environments requires agents to understand the spatial structure of previously unseen environments in order to follow language instructions. Although foundation models have opened a promising path toward zero-shot navigation without task-specific policy training, many navigators still rely on local visual cues and linear history-based reasoning, overlooking the spatial nature of navigation across explored regions, traversed paths, landmarks, and their spatial relations. In this paper, we propose SpaceVLN, a navigation agent built around Spatial Cognitive Memory and Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning. Specifically, SpaceVLN introduces an efficient stagewise closed-loop framework where planning and execution are organized around verifiable space--landmark stages. During navigation, the agent progressively abstracts explored regions into Spatial Waypoints and dynamically maintains subtask-grounded landmark evidence, forming a hierarchical Spatial Cognitive Memory for progress localization and spatial-relation understanding. Built on this memory, Spatial-CoT integrates task-progress reasoning with spatial perception, analysis, and prediction, enabling Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning for embodied navigation. The unified stage interface enables SpaceVLN to address both Vision-and-Language Navigation and Object-Goal Navigation under a unified zero-shot setting, without task-specific policy training. Across R2R-CE, RxR-CE, GN-Bench, and HM3D-OVON, SpaceVLN achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance, and real-robot deployment further validates its applicability. These results highlight Spatial Cognitive Memory and Task-Guided Spatial Reasoning as a practical foundation for stronger embodied navigation agents.

</details>


### [278] [Autonomous FPV Flight with Translational Optical Flow and Uncertainty Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09088)
*Yang Deng,Yu Hu,Feng Yu,Linzuo Zhang,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用光流分解（平移流+旋转流）和不确定性掩码的单目RGB四旋翼自主避障系统，在FPV场景下实现高达13.91 m/s（仿真）和11.79 m/s（真实世界）的飞行速度，30次真实世界测试中成功率93.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光流的端到端自主飞行方法难以从光流估计中提取最相关的信息，无法区分障碍物诱导光流与自运动背景光流，且在膨胀焦点附近信噪比低，限制了敏捷性和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 将光流分解为平移流和旋转流，仅利用包含场景几何和深度线索的平移流；2) 引入基于前向-后向光流不一致性的不确定性掩码，突出障碍物结构（包括FoE区域）；3) 将这两个线索输入到可微分仿真框架中训练的控制策略，实现感知和控制的联合一阶优化。

Result: 仿真中13.91 m/s，真实世界中11.79 m/s，真实世界30次测试成功率93.3%，几乎将此前单目RGB光流无人机避障系统的真实世界速度（6 m/s）翻倍。

Conclusion: 光流分解+不确定性掩码可以有效提升单目四旋翼在复杂环境中的自主飞行速度和鲁棒性，是部署级机器人系统的重要进步。

Abstract: Autonomous FPV quadrotor flight in complex environments using a monocular RGB camera as the sole exteroceptive sensor remains a fundamental challenge. Recent research has shown that using optical flow as the input of a neural network can achieve end-to-end autonomous flight in cluttered scenes. However, extracting the most relevant information from the flow estimation is the key bottleneck limiting agility and robustness. Existing methods struggle to disentangle obstacle-induced optical flow from the ego-motion background flow and suffer from low signal-to-noise ratios near the focus of expansion (FoE). To address these issues, we decompose the optical flow into translational and rotational components and utilize only the translational flow, which captures scene geometry and depth cues. In addition, we introduce an uncertainty mask derived from inconsistencies between forward and backward flow estimates. This mask highlights obstacle structures, including those within the FoE region. Both cues are fed to a control policy trained in a differentiable simulation framework, which enables efficient first-order optimization across perception and control. We validate our approach through extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world forest environments. The proposed system achieves robust flight at speeds of up to 13.91 m/s in simulation and 11.79 m/s in real-world tests, with a 93.3\% success rate over 30 real-world trials, nearly doubling the previously reported 6 m/s real-world speed of the monocular-RGB optical-flow UAV obstacle avoidance system.

</details>


### [279] [LAEI: Layered Autonomous Edge Intelligence Framework for Robust UAV Swarm Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09099)
*Changmin Park,Wooyong Jung,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层自主边缘智能（LAEI）框架，将无人机机载局部决策与轻量级任务级监督相结合，通过动态重关联、备份监督支持等恢复策略应对通信限制和组件故障，在仿真中降低任务完成时间并提升覆盖效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式方法存在通信瓶颈和单点故障，完全去中心化方法缺乏任务级一致性，需要一种兼顾局部自主与全局协调的可扩展无人机蜂群机制。

Method: LAEI框架：每个无人机本地执行感知、避障和动作选择，监督层提供适应性目标重分配、故障感知恢复和上下文相关的策略指导，不直接控制底层动作；包含动态重关联、备份监督支持和回退局部自主三种恢复策略。

Result: 在模拟无人机蜂群场景中，LAEI降低了任务完成时间，提升了运行效率，同时保持了碰撞感知的分布式决策能力。

Conclusion: 分层架构能有效平衡局部自主与任务级监督，在有限通信和故障场景下维持任务连续性，优于纯集中或纯去中心的方案。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV swarms require scalable coordination mechanisms that maintain mission performance under limited communication, environmental uncertainty, and component failures. Centralized approaches provide global coordination but suffer from communication bottlenecks and single-node vulnerabilities, whereas fully decentralized methods often lack mission-level consistency. This paper presents Layered Autonomous Edge Intelligence (LAEI), a UAV-swarm framework that combines onboard learned policies with lightweight mission-level supervision. Each UAV performs local perception, obstacle avoidance, and action selection onboard, while the supervisory layer provides adaptive goal reassignment, fault-aware recovery, and context-dependent policy guidance without directly controlling low-level actions. LAEI further incorporates recovery strategies, including dynamic reassociation, backup supervisory support, and fallback local autonomy, to maintain mission continuity under representative failure scenarios. We evaluate LAEI in simulated UAV-swarm scenarios using mission completion time, collision rate, and coverage efficiency. The results show that LAEI reduces mission completion time and improves operational efficiency while maintaining collision-aware distributed UAV-level decision-making.

</details>


### [280] [RAM: Reachability Across Morphologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09108)
*Tim Walter,Xinyu Chen,Jonathan Külz,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAM（Reachability Across Morphologies），一种形态条件隐式神经表示，可快速、可微地估计任意机器人形态的位姿可达性，并天然考虑自碰撞。训练使用30亿前向运动学样本，在纳秒级推理下F1=86%，比基线高14%，推理速度快三个数量级；在形态优化和轨迹优化中分别加速一个和两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作空间近似方法速度慢、精度低或局限于单一形态，缺乏一种快速、可微、跨形态通用的可达性代理函数。

Method: 构建形态条件隐式神经表示RAM，训练数据集由前向运动学生成（30亿样本），模型输出位姿可达性概率，天然包含自碰撞约束。

Result: F1=86%，纳秒级推理；相比基线F1提升14%，推理时间降低三个数量级；形态优化加速一个数量级，轨迹优化加速两个数量级。

Conclusion: RAM是一种快速、精准、跨形态泛化的可达性近似方法，可显著加速机器人形态设计与轨迹优化等下游任务。

Abstract: Many stages of the robotic lifecycle, from morphology synthesis to operation, rely fundamentally on the reachable workspace. However, current methods for approximating workspaces are slow, imprecise, or tied to a single morphology. We introduce Reachability Across Morphologies (RAM): a morphology-conditioned, implicit neural representation that acts as a fast, differentiable surrogate for pose reachability, generalising to unseen morphologies while inherently accounting for self-collisions. To train RAM, we publish a large-scale dataset of $3\cdot10^{10}$ samples generated solely from forward kinematics. Experiments show that our model achieves an $ F_1$-score of $86\%$ at nanosecond inference, outperforming the baseline by $14\%$ while reducing inference time by three orders of magnitude. We further demonstrate speed-ups of one and two orders of magnitude for gradient-based morphology and trajectory optimisation, respectively.
  Website: https://timwalter.github.io/ram.

</details>


### [281] [From USD Scenes to Knowledge Graphs: Zero-Shot Ontology Grounding with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09134)
*Jiangtao Shuai,Zongxiong Chen,Manfred Hauswirth,Sonja Schimmler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 探究LLM能否零样本自动化地将3D仿真场景中的物体映射到本体类别，在厨房场景中达到90-96%精确匹配准确率，并揭示LLM主要利用场景图中的语义线索（兄弟节点名、父路径）而非几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D仿真场景物体映射到形式化本体类别是机器人任务推理的关键瓶颈，传统依赖人工构建词典的方法脆弱且无法跨资产泛化。

Method: 使用LLM对USD场景中的物体进行零样本本体映射，通过消融实验分析语义线索（场景图中的兄弟节点名和父路径）、几何信息等不同特征的重要性。

Result: 描述性名称下LLM达到90-96%准确率，缩写名称下49-89%；匿名化语义线索后准确率降至0-6%，仅用几何信息仅4-17%。

Conclusion: LLM可有效替代人工词典实现零样本本体映射，但其能力主要依赖场景图中的语义线索而非几何推理。

Abstract: Constructing knowledge graphs from 3D simulation scenes is essential for robot task reasoning, but the key bottleneck, grounding scene objects to formal ontology classes, still relies on manually curated dictionaries that are brittle and do not generalize across assets. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this grounding step for Universal Scene Description (USD) scenes as a zero-shot, training-free alternative. On a kitchen scene (125 objects) with SOMA-HOME Ontology, LLMs achieve 90-96% exact-match accuracy with descriptive names and 49-89% with abbreviated names, substantially outperforming dictionary and embedding baselines. Under fully opaque names, context-augmented prompting recovers up to 48%. Feature ablation reveals that LLMs primarily exploit semantic cues in the scene graph (sibling names and parent paths); anonymizing these cues reduces accuracy to 0-6%, while geometry alone yields only 4-17%.

</details>


### [282] [Bridged SBI: Correcting Biased Low-Fidelity Posteriors for Cost-Efficient High-Fidelity Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09155)
*Gahee Kim,Yuki Kadokawa,Sandro M. Alcantara Tacora,Taro Abe,Daisuke Endo,Genki Yamauchi,Takeshi Hashimoto,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Bridged SBI方法，利用低成本低保真度仿真先识别粗略高密度参数区域，再通过残差桥接将低保真后验样本修正到高保真一致区域，解决粒子仿真中多保真度参数校准的偏差问题，在机器人土方工程仿真中实现更精确高效的后验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真粒子仿真参数校准计算成本极高，低保真仿真虽有速度优势但存在参数偏移导致后验有偏，现有方法无法在有限计算预算下兼顾效率与准确性。

Method: 两步策略：(1) 用低成本低保真仿真识别粗略高密度参数区域；(2) 学习局部残差桥接，通过修正低保真与高保真之间差异，将低保真后验样本向高保真一致区域迁移。

Result: 在仿真到仿真粒子参数校准和真实土壤观测的实到仿真校准实验中，Bridged SBI相比仅用高保真SBI或朴素多保真基线，在有限高保真计算成本下产生更准确可靠的后验估计。

Conclusion: 明确建模低保真-高保真差异的残差校正能有效缓解多保真SBI的后验覆盖不足问题，为计算受限的粒子仿真参数校准提供实用方案。

Abstract: Accurate calibration of particle-based simulators is crucial for robotic earthwork simulation, but analytical calibration is challenging due to this task's highly nonlinear particle dynamics and the black-box nature of conventional simulators. Although simulation-based inference (SBI) can estimate posterior distributions over simulation parameters solely from forward simulations, applying SBI directly to high-fidelity (HF) particle simulators is often computationally prohibitive. Low-fidelity (LF) simulators with coarser particles can reduce this cost, but changes in particle size and particle count shift the parameter values needed to reproduce the same observation, producing biased LF posteriors. We propose Bridged SBI, which leverages a biased but informative LF posterior to guide HF inference. This method first uses inexpensive LF simulations to identify a coarse high-density parameter region, and then it learns a local residual bridge to transport LF posterior samples toward HF-consistent regions by correcting the LF--HF discrepancy. We analyze how sequential multi-fidelity SBI (Naive-MF) can suffer from LF-induced posterior miscoverage when it directly relies on the LF posterior without discrepancy correction. We then show that Bridged SBI is designed to alleviate this issue by explicitly modeling the LF--HF discrepancy through residual correction. Experiments on both sim-to-sim particle-parameter calibration and real-to-sim calibration with real soil observation show that Bridged SBI produces more accurate and reliable HF posteriors than HF-only SBI or the Naive-MF baseline, especially under limited HF simulation costs.

</details>


### [283] [Autonomous Obstacle Removal for Excavators through Policy Learning with Particle Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09183)
*Yuki Kadokawa,Sandro M. Alcantara Tacora,Taro Abe,Daisuke Endo,Genki Yamauchi,Takeshi Hashimoto,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于埋葬条件课程学习的自主障碍物清除框架，利用粒子仿真训练策略，从RGB-D观测输出参数化挖掘轨迹，实现从仿真到真实12吨挖掘机的迁移，在3天内完成有效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主地面障碍物清除是重要的土方工程任务，但难以自动化，因为挖掘机需要随土-障碍物条件变化反复调整挖掘轨迹，而粒子仿真计算昂贵，重复挖掘进一步增加学习成本。

Method: 提出埋葬条件课程学习策略：从浅埋开始逐步增加埋深，同时调整粒子数量以控制任务难度和仿真成本；策略从RGB-D观测中提取地形和障碍物信息，输出参数化挖掘轨迹；仿真器复现真实挖掘机的观测-动作接口。

Result: 所提框架在3天内学得有效策略，基线方法训练一周仍失败；成功迁移到真实12吨挖掘机，在各种钢制障碍物上实现鲁棒清除。

Conclusion: 埋葬条件课程学习能同时控制任务难度和仿真成本，实现从仿真到真实的高效策略学习与迁移。

Abstract: Autonomous obstacle removal from the ground is an important earthwork task, but this is difficult to automate because an excavator must adapt its excavation trajectories over repeated cycles as soil-obstacle conditions change. Learning such state-dependent behavior requires a training environment that reproduces accumulated soil-obstacle interactions, including contact states, terrain deformation, and obstacle visibility. Accordingly, particle-based simulation is suitable for the relevant policy learning. However, particle simulation is computationally expensive, and repeated excavation cycles further increase the learning cost. We observe that the burial condition of an obstacle governs both task difficulty and simulation cost: deeper burial makes obstacle removal harder while also requiring more particles for accurate simulation. This observation motivates a burial-conditioned curriculum learning strategy. We propose a time-efficient sim-to-real policy learning framework in which the policy observes terrain and obstacle information from RGB-D measurements and then outputs a parameterized excavation trajectory; in this process, the simulator reproduces in a real-world excavator the same observation-action interface it uses under controllable burial conditions. The curriculum begins with shallow burial conditions and progressively increases burial depth while adjusting particle count, thus simultaneously controlling task difficulty and simulation cost. Experiments show that the proposed framework successfully learns an effective obstacle-removal policy, whereas baseline methods fail even after a full week of training. The proposed curriculum achieves effective performance within three days and achieves successful transfer to a real 12-ton excavator operating on open ground with various steel obstacles, thus demonstrating robust obstacle removal.

</details>


### [284] [Trajectory Optimization in Single and Dual-UAV Bearing-Only Target Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09188)
*Zhijian Xiao,Huayu Huang,Bin Li,Yang Shang,Banglei Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）的无人机纯方位目标定位轨迹优化方法，通过谱加权FIM目标函数和改进粒子群优化（PSO）算法，在单UAV场景中定位中位误差降低99.21%，双UAV场景提升69.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯方位目标定位中，UAV的轨迹规划直接影响观测几何构型与定位精度，现有FIM方法在退化构型附近梯度动力学差，且双UAV场景易出现轨迹聚集，需要更优的轨迹优化方法。

Method: 提出谱加权FIM目标函数改善退化构型附近的梯度动力学；针对双UAV场景引入交角正弦项优化三角测量几何；提出带运动模型约束和粒子归一化的改进PSO算法保证轨迹物理可行性。

Result: 单UAV场景中位定位误差较传统FIM方法降低99.21%，双UAV场景提升69.70%，在远程机动目标的长时纯方位定位中表现优越。

Conclusion: 提出的轨迹优化方法显著提升了纯方位目标定位精度，有效解决了退化构型下的梯度问题和双UAV轨迹聚集问题，物理可行性好。

Abstract: Bearing-only target localization is a fundamental problem in optical measurement and finds extensive applications in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) technology. Effective trajectory planning establishes favorable observation geometries, thereby enhancing the target localization accuracy of bearing-only UAV systems. This paper proposes an trajectory optimization method for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in bearing-only target localization scenarios. By leveraging the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM), the proposed approach dynamically integrates the geometric configuration and vehicle maneuverability into the optimization framework. Specifically, we introduce a spectrally-weighted FIM objective function that provides better gradient dynamics near degenerate configurations, enabling the planner to rapidly escape from poor observation conditions. For dual-UAV scenarios, an intersection angle sine term is introduced to optimize triangulation geometry by improving the sight-line intersection angle, thereby preventing trajectory aggregation. Furthermore, we propose an improved Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) algorithm with motion model constraints and particle normalization to ensure the physical feasibility of the trajectory and enhance the compatibility with the objective functions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method reduces the median localization error by 99.21% compared to conventional FIM-based approaches in single-UAV scenarios, and achieves a 69.70% improvement for dual-UAV configurations, exhibits superior performance in long-duration bearing-only target localization of maneuverability targets at extended ranges.

</details>


### [285] [Deterministic Execution of ROS~2 Applications via Lingua Franca](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09203)
*Harun Teper,Shaokai Lin,Shulu Li,Edward A. Lee,Jian-Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个框架，将未修改的ROS 2应用自动转换为Lingua Franca (LF)程序，利用逻辑时间实现确定性的回调执行顺序和一致端到端延迟，解决ROS 2发布-订阅模式固有的非确定性并发问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2的发布-订阅通信机制中回调执行顺序存在内在非确定性（单执行器内和分布式部署中均存在），导致并发问题且难以分析安全性并提供保证。

Method: 1. 识别ROS 2中可在逻辑时间下确定性执行的功能特性；2. 建立自动化转换框架，从ROS 2应用提取信息并直接转换为LF程序；3. 利用LF的逻辑时间延迟、联邦执行、故障处理等特性，在不修改ROS 2代码前提下实现确定性和时序可预测执行。

Result: 在合成示例和Autoware参考系统上验证：默认ROS 2回调执行顺序不同且端到端延迟变化，而LF控制的ROS 2系统产生确定性执行顺序和一致端到端延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效将非确定性的ROS 2应用转化为确定性执行的系统，同时保持代码无需修改，为机器人系统提供更强的安全保证和时序可预测性。

Abstract: The Robot Operating System~2 (ROS 2) is a widely used middleware for robotic systems, characterized by a publish-subscribe (pub-sub) communication mechanism in which computation is structured as callbacks dispatched by ROS 2 executors. Despite its popularity, the pub-sub pattern in ROS 2 is inherently nondeterministic: the order in which these callbacks run is nondeterministic even within a single executor, and distributed deployments add further nondeterminism from the interleaving of messages across nodes and from network latency. Such nondeterminism often leads to concurrency issues and makes it virtually impossible to analyze for safeness and provide guarantees.
  We present a framework that is able to convert an unmodified ROS 2 application and run it under Lingua Franca (LF), a coordination language for deterministic execution using logical time, so that the same input always produces the same deterministic execution order. We first describe which ROS 2 features can be executed deterministically under logical time. Such features enable the possibility to establish an automatic conversion framework to extract information from a ROS 2 application and directly convert it into an LF program. The rich features of LF, such as logical-time delays, federated execution across processes, and fault handling, can then be applied to make the ROS 2 application be executed in a deterministic and timing-predictable manner without changing the ROS 2 code. We evaluate the framework on a synthetic example and on the Autoware reference system. We show that the order in which callbacks are executed differs in default ROS 2, while also having end-to-end latencies that vary across executions. In contrast, our LF-controlled ROS 2 system produces a deterministic execution order and consistent end-to-end latencies.

</details>


### [286] [MotionWAM: Towards Foundation World Action Models for Real-Time Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09215)
*Jia Zheng,Teli Ma,Yudong Fan,Zifan Wang,Shuo Yang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionWAM是一个实时世界动作模型（WAM），通过将视频世界模型的中间去噪特征融入策略，实现了基于单目摄像头的全身类人机器人操作，采用统一运动潜空间替代传统的上下半身分离范式，在9项真实Unitree G1任务上优于VLA基线30%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM在桌面操作中表现良好，但迭代去噪过程太慢无法用于实时人形操作；同时主流层次化范式将上半身（操作策略）和下半身（基座命令跟踪）解耦，导致动作空间不一致，双腿被降级为仅维持平衡的 locomotion 工具，无法实现协调的全身运动。

Method: 提出MotionWAM：1）用统一运动潜空间替代上下半身分离，预测全身运动token覆盖行走、躯干运动、高度调节、足部交互和手部操作；2）将策略条件建立在视频世界模型的中间去噪特征上，而非用完整去噪过程；3）三阶段学习框架：先预训练视频世界模型，再适配到第一人称视觉动态，最后适配到目标人形机器人本体。

Result: 在9项真实Unitree G1任务上实时运行，整体成功率比微调的同数据VLA基线高30%以上，并能执行解耦策略无法实现的足部交互操作。

Conclusion: 视频预训练的WAM可以从桌面操作扩展到协调的类人全身人形控制，证明统一动作空间和中间特征条件化是实现实时人形操作的关键设计。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple a video dynamics prior to the policy and have shown encouraging results on tabletop manipulation, but iterative denoising over high-dimensional video-action latents leaves them too slow for real-time humanoid loco-manipulation. The problem is compounded by the dominant hierarchical paradigm, in which a high-level manipulation policy controls only the upper body while a low-level controller tracks coarse base commands -- placing upper and lower body in inconsistent action spaces and reducing the legs to balance-preserving locomotion. We present MotionWAM, a real-time WAM that drives autonomous humanoid loco-manipulation from a single egocentric camera by conditioning the policy on the intermediate denoising features of a video world model. MotionWAM replaces the upper-lower split with a unified motion latent and predicts whole-body motion tokens that jointly cover locomotion, torso motion, height regulation, foot interaction, and hand manipulation in a single action space. A three-stage learning framework progressively adapts the video world model to egocentric visual dynamics and to the target humanoid embodiment. On nine real-world Unitree G1 tasks, MotionWAM runs in real time, substantially outperforms Vision-Language-Action (VLA) baselines fine-tuned on the same demonstrations by over 30% in overall success rate, and executes task-driven foot interaction that decoupled upper-lower policies cannot reach. Our results suggest that video-pretrained WAMs can be lifted from tabletop manipulation to coordinated, human-like whole-body humanoid control.

</details>


### [287] [Self-Paced Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Superbike Racing in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09236)
*Luca Ghisi,Jacopo Essenziale,Carlo D'Eramo,Matteo Luperto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合SAC与自步课程深度强化学习(SPDL)的框架，用于在物理准确的Unity摩托车模拟器VRider SBK中训练自动驾驶摩托车赛车的智能体，解决了两轮车辆平衡与倾斜角控制的复杂性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶赛车研究主要针对四轮车辆，而摩托车因需要管理平衡、倾斜角度、更激进的转向和油门控制以及较小的重量，复杂度显著提升，但缺乏相关RL研究。

Method: 采用Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)结合Self-Paced curriculum Deep reinforcement Learning (SPDL)，动态生成渐进难度任务；状态空间包含本体感知特征、倾斜角历史以及全局赛道特征点；奖励函数鼓励赛道前进同时惩罚两轮动力学特有的不稳定行为。

Result: SPDL在训练效率、圈速和驾驶稳定性上均优于纯SAC，在多个赛道和摩托车模型上建立了RL自动驾驶摩托车赛车的首个基线。

Conclusion: SPDL结合SAC能有效处理两轮车辆平衡与倾斜角控制的复杂任务，为自动驾驶摩托车RL研究提供了可行框架和基线。

Abstract: Autonomous Racing has seen remarkable progress through deep Reinforcement Learning (RL), primarily for four-wheeled vehicles. However, motorbikes introduce substantially greater complexity due to the need to manage balance and lean angle, in addition to more reactive steering and throttle control, and a smaller weight. In this work, we present a framework for training an autonomous agent to race a superbike in VRider SBK, a physics-accurate Unity-based motorbike simulator. Our approach integrates Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with Self-Paced curriculum Deep reinforcement Learning (SPDL), which dynamically generates progressively more challenging tasks based on the agent's performance, without requiring manual curriculum design. The agent's state space comprises proprioceptive features extended with lean-angle history, along with global track features via course points. The reward signal is shaped to encourage progress along the track while penalizing instability-inducing behaviors specific to two-wheeled dynamics. Preliminary experimental results demonstrate that SPDL outperforms SAC alone in training efficiency, lap time, and driving stability across multiple tracks and motorbike models, establishing a first baseline for RL-based autonomous motorbike racing.

</details>


### [288] [Can we stabilize an inverted pendulum with feedback from a time-of-flight camera?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09237)
*Anthony Czubarow,Antonio Terpin,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明低成本、低分辨率的飞行时间相机能够提供足够反馈，用于可靠且精确地平衡倒立摆——一个经典的高速不稳定动力学基准任务，打破了此前认为该类传感器无法用于精密反馈控制的普遍认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"低分辨率ToF相机无法用于精密反馈控制"的普遍认知，探索其在快速、不稳定动力学任务中的可行性。

Method: 使用低成本、低分辨率ToF相机作为唯一反馈传感器，设计控制器实现倒立摆系统的平衡控制。

Result: ToF相机能够提供足够反馈信号，实现倒立摆的可靠且精确的平衡控制。

Conclusion: 低成本ToF相机在高速不稳定动力学控制任务中具有可行性，拓展了其在机器人精密控制中的应用前景。

Abstract: Time-of-flight cameras are popular in robotics for providing direct depth information while being compact, inexpensive, and robust to lighting conditions, but their low spatial resolution and depth noise are widely believed to preclude precise feedback control. In this paper, we show that an inexpensive, low-resolution time-of-flight camera provides sufficient feedback to reliably and precisely balance an inverted pendulum on a cart--a canonical benchmark for fast, unstable dynamics.

</details>


### [289] [RPO-PDT: Demonstrating Role-Play-Based Knowledge Adaptation for Student Support Dialogue (Demonstration System)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09255)
*Filip Janik,Ewa Olton,Robert Smales,Harris Spratt,Shea Tait,Md Zia Ullah,Yanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RPO-PDT，一个基于检索和角色扮演的对话系统，用于高等教育中的自适应学生支持，支持文本和具身交互，并通过反向角色扮演循环生成可复用的策略记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中的个人发展导师（PDT）需要提供机构特定的指导，同时兼顾角色边界、保密性和安全性，现有系统缺乏结构化知识支撑和自适应策略生成能力。

Method: 采用检索增强（retrieval-grounded）方式利用结构化知识源；设计反向角色扮演循环（reverse-roleplay loop），当对话未解决时从学生视角重放，生成替代导师策略并存入可复用策略记忆；集成显式角色、边界、保密和安全策略约束。

Result: 系统支持文本和Furhat具身体交互，能够提供安全、自适应且机构特定的学生支持对话。

Conclusion: RPO-PDT展示了通过检索增强、角色约束和反向角色扮演循环实现安全、自适应教育对话系统的可行性。
